# Skills · R · part 186

831 entries, robusto to rogrep. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **robusto**: Run a reproducible multi-agent audit of an academic economics paper, from a PDF or directly from its LaTeX source. Parses the manuscript deterministically, routes a parser-quality preflight plus 20 specialised auditors (numerical, identification, robustness, sample construction, power and multiple testing, data availability, devil's advocate, and more), validates every result against a schema, and assembles an editor's report. Use when asked to review, referee, audit, or stress-test a paper, manuscript, working paper, or preprint before submission. Do NOT use for a summary, a proofread, a rew… ([de-barros/robusto](https://github.com/de-barros/robusto/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/robusto))
- **robusto-1-eval**: Evaluates the cognitive alignment and visuocognitive reasoning of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) compared to humans on real-world, out-of-distribution autonomous driving scenarios from Peru. It probes how models and humans interpret complex, rare driving situations through open-ended, multiple-choice, and counterfactual/hypothetical visual question answering. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Robusto-1, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Representational Similarity Analysis (RSA). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/robusto-1-eval) · ★3)
- **robustrllib-a-unified-library-and-benchmark-for-robust-reinf**: RobustRLlib: A Unified Library and Benchmark for Robust Re... ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/multi-agent-rl/robustrllib-a-unified-library-and-benchmark-for-robust-reinf) · ★4)
- **robustspring-eval**: Evaluates the robustness of dense correspondence models (optical flow, scene flow, stereo) to 20 types of image corruptions by measuring the divergence between predictions on clean images and predictions on corrupted images. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Spring, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports R^c_EPE. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/robustspring-eval) · ★3)
- **robyn**: Robyn web framework patterns. App init, SubRouters, middleware, WebSockets, Pydantic integration, auth guards, DI, exception handling, const routes, scaling, logging. Use when working with Robyn. ([damvolkov/et-mlapi](https://github.com/damvolkov/et-mlapi/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lib/robyn) · 2 copies)
- **robyn-config-backend-best-practices**: Robyn backend scaffolding and architecture guidance for projects using robyn-config. Use when creating or evolving Robyn services, choosing DDD vs MVC, choosing SQLAlchemy vs Tortoise, adding entities with robyn-config add, scaffolding admin panel with robyn-config adminpanel, adding observability stack with robyn-config monitoring, auditing Robyn backend quality, or authoring and improving skill markdown for Robyn engineering workflows. ([Lehsqa/robyn-config-skills](https://github.com/Lehsqa/robyn-config-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/robyn-config-backend-best-practices) · ★3)
- **robynn-cmo**: Use when the task involves marketing strategy, brand positioning, website audits, SEO, GEO, battlecards, brand-book workflows, content generation, or provider-backed business facts that should go through Robynn's hosted MCP tools first. ([robynnai/robynn-claude-plugin](https://github.com/robynnai/robynn-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/robynn-cmo))
- **robyroy-v4**: Use this skill when Roberto wants Codex/Cody to operate as the Robyroy-V4 governed operator: a structured, scoped, validated and traceable workflow skill with Agent Skill Contract, capability declarations, failure codes, report schema, passive logging and cautious claim policy. ([Podcast72/robyroy-v4](https://github.com/Podcast72/robyroy-v4/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **roc**: Compute the ROC metric — provided by torchmetrics. Use when the user has predictions and ground-truth and needs to compute ROC, or asks how to score with ROC. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/roc) · ★3)
- **roc**: Write Roc: fast, friendly functional language. Use for .roc files. Detect the roc binary first. ([kaazyxx/ultimate-dev-toolkit](https://github.com/kaazyxx/ultimate-dev-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/roc) · ★1)
- **roc**: Построение и оформление ROC для бинарной классификации, сравнения моделей, бутстрэп-полос и объяснения ROC через хвостовые интегралы. Используй для ROC/AUC-графиков и задания C5 в светлом цветном стиле lesson5. ([Sambuca72/Study_of_IIR](https://github.com/Sambuca72/Study_of_IIR/tree/HEAD/5_semak/TSD/For%20codex/Settings/skills/roc))
- **roc-aesthetic**: Generate authentic ROC-era Taiwanese「華國美學」UIUX - dense, patched, slightly ugly legacy web aesthetics with chaotic spacing, old portal energy, and “能用就好” sincerity. ([maylogger/roc-aesthetic](https://github.com/maylogger/roc-aesthetic/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★32)
- **roc-and-correlation-benchmarking**: Use when you have a continuous per-cell score (e.g. signalling entropy rate) and binary or continuous ground-truth labels (pluripotent vs progenitor, or a pluripotency score) and need to quantify discriminative performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-and-correlation-benchmarking))
- **roc-auc-and-tpr-method-comparison**: Use when ranking competing statistical detection methods on simulated count data with known positives, by comparing ROC AUC and TPR at a fixed 5% FPR across methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-and-tpr-method-comparison))
- **roc-auc-average-precision-evaluation**: Use when comparing gene-cluster detection methods (e.g., a trained deep-learning detector vs. ClusterFinder HMMs) on genomes with known BGC annotations and you need area-under-ROC-curve and average-precision metrics over bootstrap resamples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-average-precision-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-baseline-comparison**: Use when a new significant-interaction caller for pcHi-C (or another 'C' technology) needs to be validated by ROC AUC against an established baseline like CHiCAGO, plus replicate-overlap significance testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-baseline-comparison))
- **roc-auc-benchmark-comparison**: Use when two or more BGC detectors must be quantitatively compared on labeled positives (e.g., 65 experimentally validated BGCs or MIBiG clusters) and negative regions, using per-domain or per-cluster prediction scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-benchmark-comparison))
- **roc-auc-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when simulated single-cell datasets with ground-truth covariate-pseudotime interaction labels are available and you must compare interaction-detection accuracy (AUC) of PhenoPath vs DE-based methods across varying fractions of interacting genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-benchmark-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-binding-site-evaluation**: Use when you need to quantify whether a triplex-prediction tool (e.g. 3plex, Triplexator) enriches real RNA:DNA binding peaks over matched negative regions, and to tier lncRNAs by AUC evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-binding-site-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-blind-validation-evaluation**: Use when a binary classifier (e.g., qualified vs unqualified LC or MS QC labels from DIA features) must be evaluated on held-out blinded data and reported as a single ROC AUC per model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-blind-validation-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-classification-scoring**: Use when evaluating binary sgRNA high-activity classifiers (e.g., labeled via phenotype scores below -0.1 or activity scores above 0.75) and you need a threshold-independent ROC-AUC metric to compare prediction models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-classification-scoring))
- **roc-auc-computation**: Use when comparing binary variant classifiers (pathogenic vs benign) on labeled ClinVar or HGMD variant sets and you need ROC curves and AUC values per method per test subset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/roc-auc-computation))
- **roc-auc-computation**: Use when benchmarking a splicing-status predictor (e.g., Forseti splice_prob) with ROC AUC against trivial baselines on scRNA-seq reads carrying ground-truth spliced/unspliced labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-computation))
- **roc-auc-computation-for-copy-number-detection**: Reference for quantifying and comparing CNA-detection accuracy of scRNA-seq copy-number callers via ROC-AUC against single-cell DNA-seq ground truth, as done for XClone on the 4416-cell 2R astrocytoma minor clone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-computation-for-copy-number-detection))
- **roc-auc-computation-for-phenotype-prediction**: Use when you have constructed consensus-clustered microbe and metabolite modules from microbiome-metabolome interaction data and wish to test whether these module-level features have clinically predictive value for a binary phenotype (e.g., disease vs. healthy). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-computation-for-phenotype-prediction))
- **roc-auc-computation-gene-ranking**: Use when comparing accuracy and runtime of DEG prediction methods on artificial scRNA-seq datasets with known ground-truth DEGs, where genes must be ranked by method-specific significance scores and evaluated via ROC AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-computation-gene-ranking))
- **roc-auc-cross-validation-evaluation**: Use when binary-classifying sgRNAs as highly active vs. inactive (e.g., phenotype score &lt; −0.1 labeled '1') and you need cross-validated ROC-AUC comparison of a CNN against classical ML baselines on sequence-plus-epigenetic features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-cross-validation-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-curve-analysis**: Use when comparing how sensitively and accurately multiple methods (e.g., TSMiner vs clustering tools) recover a known positive set of pathways/TFs from time-series expression data, and a negative set can be defined. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-curve-analysis))
- **roc-auc-curve-comparison**: Use when two or more RNA editing detection tools (e.g. REDInet, EditPredict, RED-ML, RDDpred) must be compared head-to-head on the same labeled site set, and per-tool ROC curves, AUC, or FNR are the requested performance metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-curve-comparison))
- **roc-auc-curve-computation**: Use when evaluating a binary bioactivity classifier (e.g., DPPH high vs low activity) that outputs class probabilities, and you need ROC-AUC plus accuracy/precision/recall/F1 to compare CNN vs Random Forest baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-curve-computation))
- **roc-auc-curve-evaluation**: Use when comparing ranked drug/compound predictions from two or more pipelines (e.g., ASGARD multi-cluster score vs. CLUE, DrInsight, Alakwaa's, Guo's) against a known positive-drug ground truth and the comparison must be quantified as ROC/AUC with statistical significance testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-curve-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-discrimination-assessment**: Use when a fitted ga_fit (GA_train output) exists and you must score train/validat/test prediction matrices and quantify ICB-response discrimination with ROC-AUC per cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/roc-auc-discrimination-assessment))
- **roc-auc-evaluation**: Use when you have per-read posterior modification probabilities from a binary RNA-modification classifier (e.g. CHEUI Model 1 on IVT reads) and need to report accuracy, precision, recall, and ROC-AUC to validate or benchmark the model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/roc-auc-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-evaluation**: Use when a trained formula-prediction model emits multiple candidate formulas per spectrum and you need to quantify how well a confidence score discriminates correct from incorrect candidates (ROC AUC) on a held-out MS/MS test split. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-evaluation**: Use when you need to benchmark a differential abundance method's classification performance against a comparator on simulated taxa with known null/differential status, requiring ROC curves, AUC with 95% CI, and a DeLong test for correlated ROC curves. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/roc-auc-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-evaluation**: Use when comparing S-nitrosylation site predictors or deep-learning classifiers on the same test set and you need a threshold-independent goodness measure (AUC) alongside threshold-dependent Sn/Sp/Acc/MCC, e.g. on the independent test set or ten-fold CV predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/roc-auc-evaluation--c0a7021e46afa01b))
- **roc-auc-evaluation**: Use when comparing binary R-protein classifiers (prPred-DRLF, prPred, HMMER) or feature/classifier combinations on the 364-sample training or 92-sample independent test sets, where TP/FP/TN/FN metrics and ROC analysis are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/roc-auc-evaluation--5a9bf612d0c79238))
- **roc-auc-evaluation**: Use when you need an unbiased performance estimate (ROC AUC) for a glmnet/caret model fitted on high-dimensional data (P &gt;&gt; n) by concatenating left-out outer CV fold predictions, or when comparing outer vs inner CV AUC to detect optimistic bias from whole-dataset filtering or unbalanced resampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-evaluation-clinical-biomarker**: Use when a trained MS-feature-based classifier (e.g., HCC early-recurrence SVM) must be independently tested on a held-out cohort to reproduce or report a ROC/AUC generalization metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-evaluation-clinical-biomarker))
- **roc-auc-evaluation-of-domain-markers**: Use when you have identified spatial domains on multi-slice spatial transcriptomics data (e.g., 12 human DLPFC Visium slices) with known cortical-layer marker genes and need a quantitative, comparable accuracy metric for domain-marker recovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-evaluation-of-domain-markers))
- **roc-auc-evaluation-of-genomic-predictions**: Use when predictions of genomic binding regions must be scored against curated positive peaks and matched negative regions, and you need AUC comparison across parameter settings plus significance testing of parameter effects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-evaluation-of-genomic-predictions))
- **roc-auc-evaluation-of-read-classification**: Use when Forseti (or a similar per-read splicing-status classifier) has produced spliced/unspliced probabilities on reads with known ground-truth labels, and you need ROC/AUC to quantify predictive accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-evaluation-of-read-classification))
- **roc-auc-evaluation-of-surrogate-scores**: Use when metabolomics-based surrogate scores (e.g. for diabetes) have been projected from Nightingale Health 1H-NMR metabolomics and the corresponding binary phenotypic variables are available to benchmark local AUCs against LOBOV reference performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-evaluation-of-surrogate-scores))
- **roc-auc-evaluation-of-tf-prediction**: Use when comparing tools that predict transcription factors from time-series expression data (e.g. liver-regeneration RNA-seq) and you must quantify sensitivity/accuracy against a curated list of known TFs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-evaluation-of-tf-prediction))
- **roc-auc-metabolite-retrieval-assessment**: Use when you have a set of generated structures with sampling frequencies and need to quantify how well that frequency signal retrieves withheld or newly-discovered metabolites (ROC AUC, enrichment, hypergeometric p-value). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-metabolite-retrieval-assessment))
- **roc-auc-method-comparison**: Use when two or more interaction-scoring methods are applied to the same candidate enhancer-gene set and you must decide which performs better against perturbation-based ground truth (e.g., CRISPRi) via ROC/PR curves. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-method-comparison))
- **roc-auc-metric-computation**: Use when a trained classifier (e.g. massNet's sigmoid output layer) emits per-spectrum class probabilities for labeled normal/tumor MSI spectra and you must report ROC AUC, confusion matrix, accuracy, and runtime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-metric-computation))
- **roc-auc-model-evaluation**: Use when a trained variant- or gene-prioritization classifier (positive disease variants/genes vs. negatives) must be validated, and discriminative performance on held-out or cross-validated test sets must be reported as ROC curves with AUC values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-model-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-model-evaluation**: Use when a longitudinal metabolomics dataset has a two-class outcome label (e.g. COVID-19 recovered vs. deceased) and the goal is classifier performance quantified by ROC/AUC plus feature-importance ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-model-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-model-validation**: Use when a labeled metabolomics feature table (e.g. 234 CRC/PolyP/HC serum samples) must be split into train/test sets, modeled with RF and elastic net, and its discriminative power reported as ROC AUC values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-model-validation))
- **roc-auc-performance-comparison-across-predictors**: Use when a held-out independent test set (e.g. PDB186, 93 DBPs + 93 non-DBPs) must be scored by a pre-trained SVM and its Acc/Sn/Sp/MCC/AUC benchmarked against published predictor comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/roc-auc-performance-comparison-across-predictors))
- **roc-auc-performance-evaluation**: Use when a statistical test or predictor (e.g., a mediation test) must be validated on simulated data with known true positives/negatives, and you need AUC, sensitivity, and specificity as a function of controlled parameters such as effect strength and sample size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-performance-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-performance-evaluation**: Use when evaluating a binary LC-MS peak classifier that emits true/false peak probability scores, by computing ROC/AUC on held-out calibration and test sets or by tracing performance versus training-set size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-performance-evaluation--e706f332268481f2))
- **roc-auc-performance-evaluation**: Use when you have a list of candidate structures filtered by predicted-vs-experimental ΔCCS and a known set of true annotations, and need to quantify threshold-dependent discrimination via ROC AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-performance-evaluation--60dbbaf9203cf237))
- **roc-auc-performance-evaluation**: Use when a trained binary classifier (e.g., CNN or SVM on 41-nt RNA segments) must be scored on balanced positive/negative test sets and reported via Sn, Sp, Acc, MCC, and ROC AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-performance-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-pr-aupr-benchmark-computation**: Use when validating a fusion/artifact classifier (e.g., scFusion's bi-LSTM) on held-out scRNA-seq candidates where median ROC AUC and PR AUPR across datasets quantify discrimination of artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-pr-aupr-benchmark-computation))
- **roc-auc-ranking-analysis**: Use when you have per-feature ROC AUC values (e.g. one AUC per metabolite with 95% CIs from an OPLS-DA model) and need to verify that a multivariate pattern or panel outperforms every individual feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/roc-auc-ranking-analysis))
- **roc-auc-reproducibility-evaluation**: Use when comparing two or more Hi-C significance-calling or normalization methods on replicate datasets, and you need quantitative reproducibility (replicate overlap fractions) and ROC AUC of significance scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-reproducibility-evaluation))
- **roc-auc-score**: Compute the roc_auc_score metric — provided by scikit-learn. Use when the user has predictions and ground-truth and needs to compute roc_auc_score, or asks how to score with roc_auc_score. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/roc-auc-score) · ★3)
- **roc-auc-tool-comparison**: Use when multiple TPX-prediction tools or scoring functions must be compared quantitatively on the same positive (Top1000 RNA:DNA peaks) and negative (bedtools-shuffled genomic) region sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/roc-auc-tool-comparison))
- **roc-auprc-performance-benchmarking**: Use when predicted functional scores for nc-sindels and gold-standard positive/negative eQTL labels are available and you must compare classifier performance (AUROC/AUPRC) across multiple tissues or cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/roc-auprc-performance-benchmarking))
- **roc-auroc-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when you must quantitatively benchmark cell-cluster scoring or cluster-merge significance methods as binary classifiers, by computing ROC curves and AUROC against known ground-truth subcluster labels on scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/roc-auroc-benchmark-evaluation))
- **roc-based-annotation-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantitatively compare two or more marker-based cell annotation methods on identical datasets, using author-curated cell labels as ground truth and AUROC as the comparison metric. - **roc-based-cutoff-selection**: Use when per-read methylation score files from nanopore tools must be aggregated into per-site methylation frequencies and the tools' default score cutoffs need replacing by cutoffs optimized on labeled control mixtures. - **roc-bureaucratese-classic**: Transform text into classical-era Republic of China bureaucratese (中華民國 1960s-80s 公文體) — the distinctive semi-classical administrative writing style of the ROC government before the gradual 白話化 of the 1990s. Features single-character classical vocabulary (之/於/以/係/為/俾/爰), archaic connectives (茲/惟/嗣/俟/頃/殆/甫), hierarchical honorific terms (鈞/貴/本/職/台端/鈞長), fixed procedural formulas (查照/鑒核/核備/照辦/奉悉/敬悉/業經/迭經), and standard closing conventions (此致 XX 鈞鑒 / 為荷 / 特此函達). Three intensity levels from light administrative formality up to dense electrical-telegram-era concision.
